# TED：词汇与搭配积累

首批选取10篇TED英文原文，保留1046个词汇义项候选。按原文段落定位，不提供虚构时间码；同词新义或独立接法保留，未标学习者掌握。

本批覆盖拖延、沟通、创造力、脆弱与关系、幸福、幽默叙事、AI、对话和毅力。原文与选择目录见[100篇TED目录](<../原文与参考资料/原文、教材与影视/TED 英语选材目录：100 个演讲与对谈.md>)。

## TED 001 · 拖延症患者的大脑

Tim Urban · 2016 · Inside the mind of a master procrastinator

### 1. major

**意思：** n. 大学主修专业

> So in college, I was a government major, which means I had to write a lot of papers.

**来源：** [Tim Urban · TED 001 · P001](https://www.ted.com/talks/tim_urban_inside_the_mind_of_a_master_procrastinator)

### 2. paper

**意思：** n. 课程论文

> So in college, I was a government major, which means I had to write a lot of papers.

**来源：** [Tim Urban · TED 001 · P001](https://www.ted.com/talks/tim_urban_inside_the_mind_of_a_master_procrastinator)

### 3. civil

**意思：** adj. 有秩序、没有闹得不可收拾

> you get started maybe a little slowly, but you get enough done in the first week that, with some heavier days later on, everything gets done, things stay civil.

**来源：** [Tim Urban · TED 001 · P003](https://www.ted.com/talks/tim_urban_inside_the_mind_of_a_master_procrastinator)

### 4. thesis

**意思：** n. 毕业论文

> But then came my 90-page senior thesis, a paper you're supposed to spend a year on.

**来源：** [Tim Urban · TED 001 · P008](https://www.ted.com/talks/tim_urban_inside_the_mind_of_a_master_procrastinator)

### 5. senior

**意思：** adj. 大学最后一年级的

> But then came my 90-page senior thesis, a paper you're supposed to spend a year on.

### 6. workflow

**意思：** n. 工作流程

**来源：** [Tim Urban · TED 001 · P008](https://www.ted.com/talks/tim_urban_inside_the_mind_of_a_master_procrastinator)

### 7. option

**意思：** n. 可行选择

### 8. bump

**意思：** v. 提高；增加

> So I'd start off light, and I'd bump it up in the middle months, and then at the end, I would kick it up into high gear just like a little staircase.

**来源：** [Tim Urban · TED 001 · P008](https://www.ted.com/talks/tim_urban_inside_the_mind_of_a_master_procrastinator)

### 9. gear

**意思：** n. 挡位，比喻工作强度

### 11. revised

**意思：** adj. 经过修改的

### 12. deadline

**意思：** n. 截止日期

> And one day I woke up with three days until the deadline, still not having written a word, and so I did the only thing I could: I wrote 90 pages over 72 hours, pulling not one but two all-nighters -- humans are not supposed to pull two all-nighters -- sprinted across campus, dove in slow motion, and got it in just at the deadline.

**来源：** [Tim Urban · TED 001 · P013](https://www.ted.com/talks/tim_urban_inside_the_mind_of_a_master_procrastinator)

## TED 002 · 怎样说话，别人才愿意听

Julian Treasure · 2013 · How to speak so that people want to listen

### 1. instrument

**意思：** n. 乐器；此处比喻人声

> The human voice: It's the instrument we all play.

### 3. habit

### 4. assemble

## TED 003 · 学校扼杀创造力吗？

Sir Ken Robinson · 2006 · Do schools kill creativity?

### 1. theme

**意思：** n. 主题

> There have been three themes running through the conference, which are relevant to what I want to talk about.

**来源：** [Sir Ken Robinson · TED 003 · P005](https://www.ted.com/talks/sir_ken_robinson_do_schools_kill_creativity)

### 2. extraordinary

**意思：** adj. 非凡的

**来源：** [Sir Ken Robinson · TED 003 · P005](https://www.ted.com/talks/sir_ken_robinson_do_schools_kill_creativity)

### 3. evidence

**意思：** n. 证据

> One is the extraordinary evidence of human creativity in all of the presentations that we've had and in all of the people here; just the variety of it and the range of it.

**来源：** [Sir Ken Robinson · TED 003 · P005](https://www.ted.com/talks/sir_ken_robinson_do_schools_kill_creativity)

### 4. creativity

**意思：** n. 创造力

> One is the extraordinary evidence of human creativity in all of the presentations that we've had and in all of the people here; just the variety of it and the range of it.

**来源：** [Sir Ken Robinson · TED 003 · P005](https://www.ted.com/talks/sir_ken_robinson_do_schools_kill_creativity)

### 5. variety

**意思：** n. 多样性

> One is the extraordinary evidence of human creativity in all of the presentations that we've had and in all of the people here; just the variety of it and the range of it.

**来源：** [Sir Ken Robinson · TED 003 · P005](https://www.ted.com/talks/sir_ken_robinson_do_schools_kill_creativity)

### 6. range

**意思：** n. 范围；种类之广

> One is the extraordinary evidence of human creativity in all of the presentations that we've had and in all of the people here; just the variety of it and the range of it.

**来源：** [Sir Ken Robinson · TED 003 · P005](https://www.ted.com/talks/sir_ken_robinson_do_schools_kill_creativity)

### 7. frankly

**意思：** adv. 坦率说

> If you're at a dinner party, and you say you work in education -- actually, you're not often at dinner parties, frankly.

**来源：** [Sir Ken Robinson · TED 003 · P006](https://www.ted.com/talks/sir_ken_robinson_do_schools_kill_creativity)

### 8. curiously

### 9. vested

**来源：** [Sir Ken Robinson · TED 003 · P014](https://www.ted.com/talks/sir_ken_robinson_do_schools_kill_creativity)

### 10. grasp

**意思：** v. 理解；把握

### 11. retire

**意思：** v. 退休

### 12. expertise

**意思：** n. 专门知识/技能

## TED 004 · 脆弱的力量

Brené Brown · 2010 · The power of vulnerability

### 1. flyer

**意思：** n. 宣传单

> And she called, and she said, "I'm really struggling with how to write about you on the little flyer." And I thought, "Well, what's the struggle?" And she said, "Well, I saw you speak, and I'm going to call you a researcher, I think, but I'm afraid if I call you a researcher, no one will come, because they'll think you're boring and irrelevant."

**来源：** [Brené Brown · TED 004 · P001](https://www.ted.com/talks/brene_brown_the_power_of_vulnerability)

### 2. irrelevant

**意思：** adj. 无关紧要的／不切题的

> And she called, and she said, "I'm really struggling with how to write about you on the little flyer." And I thought, "Well, what's the struggle?" And she said, "Well, I saw you speak, and I'm going to call you a researcher, I think, but I'm afraid if I call you a researcher, no one will come, because they'll think you're boring and irrelevant."

**来源：** [Brené Brown · TED 004 · P001](https://www.ted.com/talks/brene_brown_the_power_of_vulnerability)

### 3. planner

**意思：** n. 策划人员

> So, I'll start with this: a couple years ago, an event planner called me because I was going to do a speaking event.

**来源：** [Brené Brown · TED 004 · P001](https://www.ted.com/talks/brene_brown_the_power_of_vulnerability)

### 4. struggle

**意思：** n./v. 困难／挣扎

> And she called, and she said, "I'm really struggling with how to write about you on the little flyer." And I thought, "Well, what's the struggle?" And she said, "Well, I saw you speak, and I'm going to call you a researcher, I think, but I'm afraid if I call you a researcher, no one will come, because they'll think you're boring and irrelevant."

**来源：** [Brené Brown · TED 004 · P001](https://www.ted.com/talks/brene_brown_the_power_of_vulnerability)

### 5. insecure

**意思：** adj. 不自信的／没有安全感的

> So I think what I'll do is just call you a storyteller." And of course, the academic, insecure part of me was like, "You're going to call me a what?" And she said, "I'm going to call you a storyteller." And I was like, "Why not 'magic pixie'?"

**来源：** [Brené Brown · TED 004 · P003](https://www.ted.com/talks/brene_brown_the_power_of_vulnerability)

### 6. storyteller

**意思：** n. 讲故事的人

> And I was like, "Okay." And she said, "But the thing I liked about your talk is you're a storyteller.

**来源：** [Brené Brown · TED 004 · P003](https://www.ted.com/talks/brene_brown_the_power_of_vulnerability)

### 7. pixie

**意思：** n. 小精灵

> So I think what I'll do is just call you a storyteller." And of course, the academic, insecure part of me was like, "You're going to call me a what?" And she said, "I'm going to call you a storyteller." And I was like, "Why not 'magic pixie'?"

**来源：** [Brené Brown · TED 004 · P003](https://www.ted.com/talks/brene_brown_the_power_of_vulnerability)

## TED 005 · 什么造就美好生活？最长幸福研究的启示

Robert Waldinger · 2015 · What makes a good life? Lessons from the longest study on happiness

### 1. millennial

**意思：** n. 千禧一代成员

> There was a recent survey of millennials asking them what their most important life goals were, and over 80 percent said that a major life goal for them was to get rich.

**来源：** [Robert Waldinger · TED 005 · P001](https://www.ted.com/talks/robert_waldinger_what_makes_a_good_life_lessons_from_the_longest_study_on_happiness)

### 2. survey

**意思：** n. 调查

> There was a recent survey of millennials asking them what their most important life goals were, and over 80 percent said that a major life goal for them was to get rich.

**来源：** [Robert Waldinger · TED 005 · P001](https://www.ted.com/talks/robert_waldinger_what_makes_a_good_life_lessons_from_the_longest_study_on_happiness)

### 3. goal

**意思：** n. 目标

> There was a recent survey of millennials asking them what their most important life goals were, and over 80 percent said that a major life goal for them was to get rich.

**来源：** [Robert Waldinger · TED 005 · P001](https://www.ted.com/talks/robert_waldinger_what_makes_a_good_life_lessons_from_the_longest_study_on_happiness)

### 4. impression

**意思：** n. 印象／观念

> We're given the impression that these are the things that we need to go after in order to have a good life.

**来源：** [Robert Waldinger · TED 005 · P003](https://www.ted.com/talks/robert_waldinger_what_makes_a_good_life_lessons_from_the_longest_study_on_happiness)

### 5. hindsight

**意思：** n. 事后认识／回头看的判断

> Most of what we know about human life we know from asking people to remember the past, and as we know, hindsight is anything but 20/20.

**来源：** [Robert Waldinger · TED 005 · P003](https://www.ted.com/talks/robert_waldinger_what_makes_a_good_life_lessons_from_the_longest_study_on_happiness)

### 6. 20/20

**意思：** adj./表达 完全清晰准确的

> Most of what we know about human life we know from asking people to remember the past, and as we know, hindsight is anything but 20/20.

**来源：** [Robert Waldinger · TED 005 · P003](https://www.ted.com/talks/robert_waldinger_what_makes_a_good_life_lessons_from_the_longest_study_on_happiness)

**补充：** 原指标准视力；这里比喻事后判断清楚。

### 7. vast

**意思：** adj. 大量的／广大的

> We forget vast amounts of what happens to us in life, and sometimes memory is downright creative.

**来源：** [Robert Waldinger · TED 005 · P003](https://www.ted.com/talks/robert_waldinger_what_makes_a_good_life_lessons_from_the_longest_study_on_happiness)

### 8. downright

**意思：** adv. 简直／彻头彻尾

> We forget vast amounts of what happens to us in life, and sometimes memory is downright creative.

**来源：** [Robert Waldinger · TED 005 · P003](https://www.ted.com/talks/robert_waldinger_what_makes_a_good_life_lessons_from_the_longest_study_on_happiness)

### 9. creative

**意思：** adj. 会创造／编造的

> We forget vast amounts of what happens to us in life, and sometimes memory is downright creative.

**来源：** [Robert Waldinger · TED 005 · P003](https://www.ted.com/talks/robert_waldinger_what_makes_a_good_life_lessons_from_the_longest_study_on_happiness)

**补充：** 此处幽默说记忆会添油加醋。

### 10. constantly

**意思：** adv. 不断地／总是

> And we're constantly told to lean in to work, to push harder and achieve more.

**来源：** [Robert Waldinger · TED 005 · P003](https://www.ted.com/talks/robert_waldinger_what_makes_a_good_life_lessons_from_the_longest_study_on_happiness)

### 11. choice

**意思：** n. 选择

> Pictures of entire lives, of the choices that people make and how those choices work out for them, those pictures are almost impossible to get.

**来源：** [Robert Waldinger · TED 005 · P003](https://www.ted.com/talks/robert_waldinger_what_makes_a_good_life_lessons_from_the_longest_study_on_happiness)

### 12. unfold

**意思：** v. 逐渐展开／发生

> But what if we could watch entire lives as they unfold through time?

**来源：** [Robert Waldinger · TED 005 · P004](https://www.ted.com/talks/robert_waldinger_what_makes_a_good_life_lessons_from_the_longest_study_on_happiness)

### 13. development

**意思：** n. 发展／成长

> The Harvard Study of Adult Development may be the longest study of adult life that's ever been done.

**来源：** [Robert Waldinger · TED 005 · P005](https://www.ted.com/talks/robert_waldinger_what_makes_a_good_life_lessons_from_the_longest_study_on_happiness)

### 14. track

**意思：** v. 长期跟踪观察

> For 75 years, we've tracked the lives of 724 men, year after year, asking about their work, their home lives, their health, and of course asking all along the way without knowing how their life stories were going to turn out.

**来源：** [Robert Waldinger · TED 005 · P005](https://www.ted.com/talks/robert_waldinger_what_makes_a_good_life_lessons_from_the_longest_study_on_happiness)

### 15. exceedingly

**意思：** adv. 极其

> Studies like this are exceedingly rare.

**来源：** [Robert Waldinger · TED 005 · P006](https://www.ted.com/talks/robert_waldinger_what_makes_a_good_life_lessons_from_the_longest_study_on_happiness)

### 16. funding

**意思：** n. 资金资助

> Almost all projects of this kind fall apart within a decade because too many people drop out of the study, or funding for the research dries up, or the researchers get distracted, or they die, and nobody moves the ball further down the field.

**来源：** [Robert Waldinger · TED 005 · P006](https://www.ted.com/talks/robert_waldinger_what_makes_a_good_life_lessons_from_the_longest_study_on_happiness)

### 17. persistence

**意思：** n. 坚持／持续投入

> But through a combination of luck and the persistence of several generations of researchers, this study has survived.

**来源：** [Robert Waldinger · TED 005 · P006](https://www.ted.com/talks/robert_waldinger_what_makes_a_good_life_lessons_from_the_longest_study_on_happiness)

### 18. original

**意思：** adj. 最初的

### 54. community

**意思：** n. 社区／共同体

### 55. committed

**意思：** adj. 稳定投入、认真的

> And we know that you can be lonely in a crowd and you can be lonely in a marriage, so the second big lesson that we learned is that it's not just the number of friends you have, and it's not whether or not you're in a committed relationship, but it's the quality of your close relationships that matters.

**意思：** n. 人群

**来源：** [Robert Waldinger · TED 005 · P016](https://www.ted.com/talks/robert_waldinger_what_makes_a_good_life_lessons_from_the_longest_study_on_happiness)

## TED 011 · 让对话更好的十种方法

Celeste Headlee · 2015 · 10 ways to have a better conversation

### 1. unfriend

**意思：** v. 在社交平台取消好友关系

> All right, I want to see a show of hands: how many of you have unfriended someone on Facebook because they said something offensive about politics or religion, childcare, food?

**来源：** [Celeste Headlee · TED 011 · P001](https://www.ted.com/talks/celeste_headlee_10_ways_to_have_a_better_conversation)

### 2. offensive

**意思：** adj. 冒犯人的，令人不快的

> All right, I want to see a show of hands: how many of you have unfriended someone on Facebook because they said something offensive about politics or religion, childcare, food?

**来源：** [Celeste Headlee · TED 011 · P001](https://www.ted.com/talks/celeste_headlee_10_ways_to_have_a_better_conversation)

### 3. childcare

**意思：** n. 儿童照护／育儿

> All right, I want to see a show of hands: how many of you have unfriended someone on Facebook because they said something offensive about politics or religion, childcare, food?

**来源：** [Celeste Headlee · TED 011 · P001](https://www.ted.com/talks/celeste_headlee_10_ways_to_have_a_better_conversation)

### 4. politics

**意思：** n. 政治议题

> All right, I want to see a show of hands: how many of you have unfriended someone on Facebook because they said something offensive about politics or religion, childcare, food?

**来源：** [Celeste Headlee · TED 011 · P001](https://www.ted.com/talks/celeste_headlee_10_ways_to_have_a_better_conversation)

**意思：** n. 宗教

> All right, I want to see a show of hands: how many of you have unfriended someone on Facebook because they said something offensive about politics or religion, childcare, food?

**来源：** [Celeste Headlee · TED 011 · P001](https://www.ted.com/talks/celeste_headlee_10_ways_to_have_a_better_conversation)

### 6. avoid

**意思：** v. 刻意避开

> And how many of you know at least one person that you avoid because you just don't want to talk to them?

**来源：** [Celeste Headlee · TED 011 · P003](https://www.ted.com/talks/celeste_headlee_10_ways_to_have_a_better_conversation)

### 7. polite

**意思：** adj. 礼貌的

> You know, it used to be that in order to have a polite conversation, we just had to follow the advice of Henry Higgins in "My Fair Lady": Stick to the weather and your health.

**来源：** [Celeste Headlee · TED 011 · P005](https://www.ted.com/talks/celeste_headlee_10_ways_to_have_a_better_conversation)

### 8. anti-vaxxing

**意思：** n. 反对接种疫苗的主张或行为

> But these days, with climate change and anti-vaxxing, those subjects --

### 9. climate

**意思：** n. 气候

> But these days, with climate change and anti-vaxxing, those subjects --

**来源：** [Celeste Headlee · TED 011 · P005](https://www.ted.com/talks/celeste_headlee_10_ways_to_have_a_better_conversation)

### 10. devolve

**意思：** v. 退化；向坏的局面演变

> So this world that we live in, this world in which every conversation has the potential to devolve into an argument, where our politicians can't speak to one another and where even the most trivial of issues have someone fighting both passionately for it and against it, it's not normal.

**来源：** [Celeste Headlee · TED 011 · P007](https://www.ted.com/talks/celeste_headlee_10_ways_to_have_a_better_conversation)

### 11. trivial

**来源：** [Celeste Headlee · TED 011 · P007](https://www.ted.com/talks/celeste_headlee_10_ways_to_have_a_better_conversation)

**意思：** adj. 简短的

**来源：** [Celeste Headlee · TED 011 · P040](https://www.ted.com/talks/celeste_headlee_10_ways_to_have_a_better_conversation)

### 102. ritual

**意思：** n. 惯常的仪式性做法

> You know, I grew up with a very famous grandfather, and there was kind of a ritual in my home.

**来源：** [Celeste Headlee · TED 011 · P041](https://www.ted.com/talks/celeste_headlee_10_ways_to_have_a_better_conversation)

### 103. runner-up

**意思：** n. 亚军；次优胜者

> She was the runner-up to Miss America.

**来源：** [Celeste Headlee · TED 011 · P041](https://www.ted.com/talks/celeste_headlee_10_ways_to_have_a_better_conversation)

### 104. mayor

**意思：** n. 市长

> He was the mayor of Sacramento.

**来源：** [Celeste Headlee · TED 011 · P041](https://www.ted.com/talks/celeste_headlee_10_ways_to_have_a_better_conversation)

### 105. hidden

**意思：** adj. 隐藏的、不为人知的

> And I kind of grew up assuming everyone has some hidden, amazing thing about them.

**来源：** [Celeste Headlee · TED 011 · P041](https://www.ted.com/talks/celeste_headlee_10_ways_to_have_a_better_conversation)

### 106. amazed

**意思：** adj. 惊喜／惊讶的

> And I’m always prepared to be amazed, and I'm never disappointed.

**来源：** [Celeste Headlee · TED 011 · P041](https://www.ted.com/talks/celeste_headlee_10_ways_to_have_a_better_conversation)

### 107. disappointed

**意思：** adj. 失望的

> And I’m always prepared to be amazed, and I'm never disappointed.

**来源：** [Celeste Headlee · TED 011 · P041](https://www.ted.com/talks/celeste_headlee_10_ways_to_have_a_better_conversation)

## TED 013 · 连接着，却孤独着？

Sherry Turkle · 2012 · Connected, but alone?

### 1. text

**意思：** n. 短信／文字消息

> Her text said, "Mom, you will rock."

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P001](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 2. rock

**意思：** v. 表现出色，干得很棒

> Her text said, "Mom, you will rock."

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P001](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

**补充：** 口语鼓励。

### 3. embody

**意思：** v. 体现；成为某种概念的具体实例

> I embody the central paradox.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P001](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 4. paradox

**意思：** n. 看似矛盾却并存的情况

> I embody the central paradox.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P001](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 5. hug

**意思：** n. 拥抱

> Getting that text was like getting a hug.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P001](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 6. central

**意思：** adj. 核心的

> I embody the central paradox.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P001](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 7. heady

**意思：** adj. 令人兴奋陶醉的

> In those heady days, we were experimenting with chat rooms and online virtual communities.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P002](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 8. virtual

**意思：** adj. 虚拟的，网络模拟的

> In those heady days, we were experimenting with chat rooms and online virtual communities.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P002](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 9. aspect

**意思：** n. 方面，侧面

> We were exploring different aspects of ourselves.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P002](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 10. unplug

**意思：** v. 断开网络／设备连接

> And then we unplugged.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P002](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

**补充：** 此处从网络生活抽身。

### 11. reminder

**意思：** n. 让人想起某事的提示／事物

> Actually that reminder of my daughter brings me to the beginning of my story.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P002](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 12. explore

**意思：** v. 探索，探究

> We were exploring different aspects of ourselves.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P002](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 13. case

**意思：** n. 论证／支持某立场的理由

> I'm still excited by technology, but I believe, and I'm here to make the case, that we're letting it take us places that we don't want to go.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P003](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 14. device

**意思：** n. 电子设备

> And what I've found is that our little devices, those little devices in our pockets, are so psychologically powerful that they don't only change what we do, they change who we are.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P004](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 15. psychologically

**意思：** adv. 在心理方面

> And what I've found is that our little devices, those little devices in our pockets, are so psychologically powerful that they don't only change what we do, they change who we are.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P004](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 16. odd

**意思：** adj. 奇怪的，不寻常的

> Some of the things we do now with our devices are things that, only a few years ago, we would have found odd or disturbing, but they've quickly come to seem familiar, just how we do things.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P004](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 17. disturbing

**意思：** adj. 令人不安的

> Some of the things we do now with our devices are things that, only a few years ago, we would have found odd or disturbing, but they've quickly come to seem familiar, just how we do things.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P004](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 18. powerful

**意思：** adj. 有巨大影响力的

> And what I've found is that our little devices, those little devices in our pockets, are so psychologically powerful that they don't only change what we do, they change who we are.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P004](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 19. corporate

**意思：** adj. 公司的／企业的

> So just to take some quick examples: People text or do email during corporate board meetings.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P005](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 20. presentation

**意思：** n. 报告／演示

> They text and shop and go on Facebook during classes, during presentations, actually during all meetings.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P005](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 21. deny

**意思：** v. 不把应得的东西给予某人

> But then these same children deny each other their full attention.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P005](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

**补充：** deny sb. sth.；此处不是否认义。

### 22. shot

**意思：** n. 照片

> This is a recent shot of my daughter and her friends being together while not being together.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P005](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

**补充：** 不是射击义。

### 23. funeral

**意思：** n. 葬礼

> And we even text at funerals.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P005](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 24. revery

**意思：** n. 沉思／遐想

> We remove ourselves from our grief or from our revery and we go into our phones.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P005](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

**补充：** 原文拼写，亦作reverie。

### 25. attention

**意思：** n. 注意力／关注

> Parents text and do email at breakfast and at dinner while their children complain about not having their parents' full attention.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P005](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 26. remove

**意思：** v. 使离开／抽离

> We remove ourselves from our grief or from our revery and we go into our phones.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P005](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 27. self-reflection

**意思：** n. 自我反思

> It matters to me because I think we're setting ourselves up for trouble -- trouble certainly in how we relate to each other, but also trouble in how we relate to ourselves and our capacity for self-reflection.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P006](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 28. elsewhere

**意思：** adv. 在别处

> People want to be with each other, but also elsewhere -- connected to all the different places they want to be.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P006](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 29. customize

**意思：** v. 按自己的需要调整／定制

> People want to customize their lives.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P006](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 30. matter

**意思：** v. 重要；产生影响

> Why does this matter?

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P006](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 31. control

**意思：** n. 控制权

> They want to go in and out of all the places they are because the thing that matters most to them is control over where they put their attention.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P006](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 32. lament

**意思：** v. 感叹／哀叹

> A 50-year-old business man lamented to me that he feels he doesn't have colleagues anymore at work.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P007](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 33. colleague

**意思：** n. 同事

> A 50-year-old business man lamented to me that he feels he doesn't have colleagues anymore at work.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P007](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 34. interrupt

**意思：** v. 打断，打扰

> And he says he doesn't want to interrupt his colleagues because, he says, "They're too busy on their email."

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P007](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 35. executive

**意思：** n. 企业管理人员／高管

> But what might feel just right for that middle-aged executive can be a problem for an adolescent who needs to develop face-to-face relationships.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P008](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 36. adolescent

**意思：** n. 青少年

> But what might feel just right for that middle-aged executive can be a problem for an adolescent who needs to develop face-to-face relationships.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P008](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 37. wistfully

**意思：** adv. 带着遗憾或向往地

> An 18-year-old boy who uses texting for almost everything says to me wistfully, "Someday, someday, but certainly not now, I'd like to learn how to have a conversation."

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P008](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 38. amount

**意思：** n. 数量／分量

> Across the generations, I see that people can't get enough of each other, if and only if they can have each other at a distance, in amounts they can control.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P008](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 39. edit

**意思：** v. 编辑，修改内容

> We get to edit, and that means we get to delete, and that means we get to retouch, the face, the voice, the flesh, the body -- not too little, not too much, just right.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P009](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 40. retouch

**意思：** v. 修饰／修图润色

> We get to edit, and that means we get to delete, and that means we get to retouch, the face, the voice, the flesh, the body -- not too little, not too much, just right.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P009](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 41. flesh

**意思：** n. 肉体／身体的实体部分

> We get to edit, and that means we get to delete, and that means we get to retouch, the face, the voice, the flesh, the body -- not too little, not too much, just right.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P009](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 42. post

**意思：** v. 在网络发布内容

> Texting, email, posting, all of these things let us present the self as we want to be.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P009](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 43. present

**意思：** v. 展示／呈现

> Texting, email, posting, all of these things let us present the self as we want to be.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P009](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

**补充：** 与形容词present区分。

### 44. rich

**意思：** adj. 丰富、多层次的

> Human relationships are rich and they're messy and they're demanding.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P010](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

**补充：** 不是有钱义。

### 45. demanding

**意思：** adj. 要求高、需要投入很多的

> Human relationships are rich and they're messy and they're demanding.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P010](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 46. sacrifice

**意思：** v. 舍弃一种价值以换取另一种

> And when we do, one of the things that can happen is that we sacrifice conversation for mere connection.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P010](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 47. mere

**意思：** adj. 仅仅的；不过是

> And when we do, one of the things that can happen is that we sacrifice conversation for mere connection.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P010](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 48. short-change

**意思：** v. 使获得的少于应得的；亏待

> We short-change ourselves.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P010](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

**补充：** 源自少找钱，此处比喻义。

### 49. connection

**意思：** n. 连接／保持联络

> And when we do, one of the things that can happen is that we sacrifice conversation for mere connection.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P010](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

**补充：** 此处与深入conversation对照。

### 50. sip

**意思：** n. 一小口；比喻零碎交流

> He said, "Don't all those little tweets, don't all those little sips of online communication, add up to one big gulp of real conversation?"

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P011](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 51. gulp

**意思：** n. 一大口

> He said, "Don't all those little tweets, don't all those little sips of online communication, add up to one big gulp of real conversation?"

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P011](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 52. discrete

**意思：** adj. 各自独立、分开的

> Connecting in sips may work for gathering discrete bits of information, they may work for saying, "I'm thinking about you," or even for saying, "I love you," -- I mean, look at how I felt when I got that text from my daughter -- but they don't really work for learning about each other, for really coming to know and understand each other.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P011](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 53. flight

**意思：** n. 逃离／回避

> So a flight from conversation can really matter because it can compromise our capacity for self-reflection.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P011](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

**补充：** 不是乘机旅行义。

### 54. compromise

**意思：** v. 损害，削弱

> So a flight from conversation can really matter because it can compromise our capacity for self-reflection.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P011](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

**补充：** 不是妥协义。

### 55. bedrock

**意思：** n. 根基，坚实基础

> For kids growing up, that skill is the bedrock of development.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P011](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

**补充：** 比喻义。

### 56. gather

**意思：** v. 收集

> Connecting in sips may work for gathering discrete bits of information, they may work for saying, "I'm thinking about you," or even for saying, "I love you," -- I mean, look at how I felt when I got that text from my daughter -- but they don't really work for learning about each other, for really coming to know and understand each other.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P011](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 57. altogether

**意思：** adv. 完全，彻底

> And what I'm seeing is that people get so used to being short-changed out of real conversation, so used to getting by with less, that they've become almost willing to dispense with people altogether.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P012](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 58. version

**意思：** n. 版本

> So for example, many people share with me this wish, that some day a more advanced version of Siri, the digital assistant on Apple's iPhone, will be more like a best friend, someone who will listen when others won't.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P012](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 59. reflect

**意思：** v. 反映，体现

> I believe this wish reflects a painful truth that I've learned in the past 15 years.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P012](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 60. appealing

**意思：** adj. 有吸引力的

> That's why it's so appealing to have a Facebook page or a Twitter feed -- so many automatic listeners.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P012](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 61. feed

**意思：** n. 社交平台动态流

> That's why it's so appealing to have a Facebook page or a Twitter feed -- so many automatic listeners.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P012](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

**补充：** 不是喂养义。

### 62. willing

**意思：** adj. 愿意的

> And what I'm seeing is that people get so used to being short-changed out of real conversation, so used to getting by with less, that they've become almost willing to dispense with people altogether.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P012](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 63. automatic

**意思：** adj. 自动的，无需主动招揽的

> That's why it's so appealing to have a Facebook page or a Twitter feed -- so many automatic listeners.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P012](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 64. sociable

**意思：** adj. 善于社交／能互动的

> We're developing robots, they call them sociable robots, that are specifically designed to be companions -- to the elderly, to our children, to us.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P013](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

**补充：** 此处机器人类别称谓。

### 65. companion

**意思：** n. 陪伴者／伙伴

> We're developing robots, they call them sociable robots, that are specifically designed to be companions -- to the elderly, to our children, to us.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P013](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 66. elderly

**意思：** adj./n. 年长的；the elderly指老年人群

> We're developing robots, they call them sociable robots, that are specifically designed to be companions -- to the elderly, to our children, to us.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P013](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 67. nursing home

**意思：** n. 养老护理机构

> During my research I worked in nursing homes, and I brought in these sociable robots that were designed to give the elderly the feeling that they were understood.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P013](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 68. seal

**意思：** n. 海豹

> And one day I came in and a woman who had lost a child was talking to a robot in the shape of a baby seal.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P013](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

**补充：** 不是密封或印章义。

### 69. comfort

**意思：** v. 安慰

> It comforted her.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P013](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 70. specifically

**意思：** adv. 专门地

> We're developing robots, they call them sociable robots, that are specifically designed to be companions -- to the elderly, to our children, to us.

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P013](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 71. confidence

**意思：** n. 信心，确信

> Have we so lost confidence that we will be there for each other?

**来源：** [Sherry Turkle · TED 013 · P013](https://www.ted.com/talks/sherry_turkle_connected_but_alone)

### 72. arc

**意思：** n. 发展轨迹／完整历程

> But that woman was trying to make sense of her life with a machine that had no experience of the arc of a human life.

## TED 020 · 坚毅：热情与坚持的力量

Angela Lee Duckworth · 2013 · Grit: The power of passion and perseverance

### 1. consulting

**意思：** n. 咨询业务

> When I was 27 years old, I left a very demanding job in management consulting for a job that was even more demanding: teaching.

**来源：** [Angela Lee Duckworth · TED 020 · P001](https://www.ted.com/talks/angela_lee_duckworth_grit_the_power_of_passion_and_perseverance)

### 2. grader

**意思：** n. 某年级的学生

> I went to teach seventh graders math in the New York City public schools.

**来源：** [Angela Lee Duckworth · TED 020 · P001](https://www.ted.com/talks/angela_lee_duckworth_grit_the_power_of_passion_and_perseverance)

### 3. quiz

**意思：** n. 小测验

> And like any teacher, I made quizzes and tests.

### 4. assignment

**意思：** n. 布置的任务／作业

> I gave out homework assignments.

### 5. calculate

**意思：** v. 计算

> When the work came back, I calculated grades.

**意思：** n. 成绩／评分

**意思：** adj. 公立的
